<section class="section import-section">
<div class="container">
<div class="content">
<h2>Import your team to Chanty</h2>
<p>Import feature gives you an opportunity to transfer your message history and other team data from a third-party messenger directly to Chanty.</p>
<strong>We support import from:</strong>

  // 2. This code loads the IFrame Player API code asynchronously.
  var tag = document.createElement('script');

  tag.src = "https://www.youtube.com/iframe_api";
  var firstScriptTag = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
  firstScriptTag.parentNode.insertBefore(tag, firstScriptTag);

  // 3. This function creates an <iframe> (and YouTube player)
  //    after the API code downloads.
  var player;

  document.getElementById("watch-video-opener").addEventListener("click", function onYouTubeIframeAPIReady() {
    player = new YT.Player('player', {
      height: '480',
      width: '800',
      videoId: 'vSbospFNn1o',
      events: {
        'onReady': onPlayerReady,
        'onStateChange': onPlayerStateChange
      }
    });
  });
  document.getElementById("watch-wideo-latop").addEventListener("click", function onYouTubeIframeAPIReady() {
    player = new YT.Player('player', {
      height: '480',
      width: '800',
      videoId: 'vSbospFNn1o',
      events: {
        'onReady': onPlayerReady,
        'onStateChange': onPlayerStateChange
      }
    });
  });

  // 4. The API will call this function when the video player is ready.
  function onPlayerReady(event) {
    event.target.playVideo();
  }

  // 5. The API calls this function when the player's state changes.
  //    The function indicates that when playing a video (state=1),
  //    the player should play for six seconds and then stop.
  var done = false;
  function onPlayerStateChange(event) {
    if (event.data == YT.PlayerState.PLAYING && !done) {
      document.getElementById("close-video-popup").addEventListener("click", function() {player.destroy();});
      document.getElementById("blackout").addEventListener("click", function() {player.destroy();});
      // done = false;
    }
  }
  document.getElementById("close-video-popup").addEventListener("click", function() {
    player.destroy();
    document.getElementById("video-popup").setAttribute("style", "display: none;");
    document.getElementById("blackout").setAttribute("style", "display: none;");
  });
  document.getElementById("blackout").addEventListener("click", function() {
    player.destroy();
    document.getElementById("video-popup").setAttribute("style", "display: none;");
    document.getElementById("blackout").setAttribute("style", "display: none;");
  });
</script>
